{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_01JB157j4JVQXmUrnnbdM7ze","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01Rs4bqgXs3L13dvF4KxEeK1","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/environment/skills/qutip-basics.md","content":"# QuTiP Quantum Computing Library - Basics\n\n## Overview\nQuTiP (Quantum Toolbox in Python) is a comprehensive open-source library for simulating the dynamics of open quantum systems. It provides tools for solving master equations, calculating quantum properties, and working with quantum states and operators.\n\n## Installation\n```bash\npip install qutip numpy scipy\n```\n\n## Core Concepts\n\n### Quantum Objects (Qobj)\nQuTiP uses the `Qobj` class to represent quantum states and operators:\n```python\nimport qutip as qt\n\n# Create a Fock state (photon number basis)\nstate = qt.fock(10, 3)  # 10-dimensional space, 3rd state\n\n# Create operators\na = qt.destroy(10)      # Annihilation operator\na_dag = qt.create(10)   # Creation operator\nN = a_dag * a           # Number operator\n\n# Pauli operators for qubits\nsx = qt.sigmax()\nsy = qt.sigmay()\nsz = qt.sigmaz()\nsp = qt.sigmap()        # Raising operator |0><1|\nsm = qt.sigmam()        # Lowering operator |1><0|\n```\n\n### Composite Systems (Tensor Products)\n```python\n# Create operators for composite systems\nH = qt.tensor(sx, sz)   # Two-qubit system\ncavity_ops = qt.tensor(a, qt.qeye(2))  # Cavity + qubit\n```\n\n### Liouvillian and Master Equations\nThe master equation in Lindblad form describes open quantum systems:\n```python\n# Hamiltonian\nH = omega * qt.sigmaz() / 2\n\n# Collapse operators (dissipation channels)\nc_ops = [gamma * qt.sigmam()]  # Spontaneous emission\n\n# Create Liouvillian (superoperator)\nL = qt.liouvillian(H, c_ops)\n```\n\n## Steady State Calculation\n\n### Method 1: Iterative Solver\n```python\n# Find steady state (most efficient for large systems)\nrho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops)\n```\n\n### Method 2: Direct Eigenvalue Solution\n```python\n# Solve as eigenvalue problem: L*rho = 0\nimport numpy as np\nevals, evecs = qt.eigen(L)\nrho_ss = qt.Qobj(evecs[:, 0].reshape(L.dims[0]),\n                   dims=L.dims[0], shape=L.shape)\n```\n\n## Key Parameters\n- `N`: Dimension of Hilbert space\n- `c_ops`: List of collapse operators [γ₁√(c₁), γ₂√(c₂), ...]\n- `options`: Solver options (can set accuracy, iterations)\n\n## Useful Methods\n```python\n# Extract expectation values\nexpect_val = qt.expect(operator, state)\n\n# Partial trace (trace out subsystem)\nrho_reduced = qt.ptrace(rho, mask=[True, False])  # Keep first subsystem\n\n# Trace\ntrace_val = qt.tr(state)\n\n# Operator properties\nnorm = qt.norm(state)\nentropy = qt.entropy_vn(state)  # Von Neumann entropy\n```\n\n## Reference\n- Official docs: http://qutip.org\n- Master Equation: Lindblad form equation in open quantum systems theory\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":273,"cache_read_input_tokens":19183,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":273,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e0305cd3-9616-4b8b-9c56-e058f1374831","uuid":"3fba1f18-6d60-4416-8ac4-123aa3842059"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_015ACjM9kmKsexJdpk8iyZzr","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01CJumekZXezjeK9LDcyat7P","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/environment/skills/wigner-function.md","content":"# Wigner Function Calculation and Visualization\n\n## Overview\nThe Wigner function is a quasi-probability distribution that represents quantum states in phase space. For a quantum harmonic oscillator (cavity mode), it's defined over position (x) and momentum (p) quadratures.\n\n## Mathematical Background\n\nFor a cavity mode with annihilation operator `a`:\n- Position (x) quadrature: `x = (a + a†)/√2`\n- Momentum (p) quadrature: `p = i(a† - a)/√2`\n\nThe Wigner function W(x,p) is a quasi-probability distribution in phase space that can take negative values (indicating non-classical states).\n\n## Using QuTiP for Wigner Function\n\n### Basic Calculation\n```python\nimport qutip as qt\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Define grid for phase space\nxvec = np.linspace(-6, 6, 100)\npvec = np.linspace(-6, 6, 100)\n\n# Calculate Wigner function for a quantum state\nstate = qt.coherent(10, 2)  # Coherent state\nW = qt.wigner(state, xvec, pvec)\n# W is a 2D array: shape (len(pvec), len(xvec))\n```\n\n### High-Resolution Calculation\n```python\n# For publication-quality results\nxvec = np.linspace(-6, 6, 1000)\npvec = np.linspace(-6, 6, 1000)\n\n# Calculate Wigner function\n# This may take time for large grids\nW = qt.wigner(state, xvec, pvec)\n```\n\n### Key Parameters\n- `state`: Quantum state (Qobj)\n- `xvec`: Position quadrature values (1D array)\n- `pvec`: Momentum quadrature values (1D array)\n- Returns: 2D numpy array W[i,j] where i indexes pvec, j indexes xvec\n\n## Visualization and Analysis\n\n### Properties of Wigner Function\n```python\n# The Wigner function contains full information about the quantum state\n# Useful properties:\n# - For Gaussian states (coherent, squeezed), it's always positive\n# - For non-classical states, it can have negative values (sign of non-classicality)\n# - The marginal distribution gives the quadrature probabilities\n\n# Marginal distributions\nx_margin = np.trapz(W, pvec[:, np.newaxis], axis=0)\np_margin = np.trapz(W, xvec[np.newaxis, :], axis=1)\n```\n\n### Normalization\n```python\n# Wigner function should integrate to 1\nintegral = np.trapz(np.trapz(W, pvec, axis=0), xvec)\nprint(f\"Integral check: {integral}\")  # Should be ~1.0\n```\n\n## Performance Considerations\n\n### Memory and Computation\n- 1000×1000 grid creates 1M data points\n- Wigner function calculation is O(N²) in grid size\n- For cavity with n_max=16: manageable but may take several seconds per state\n- Consider using FFT-based methods for very large grids\n\n### Algorithm Options in QuTiP\n```python\n# Method parameter (default: 'iterative')\n# 'iterative': Uses Moyal's formula (default, good for most cases)\n# Can also request other methods if available\n\nW = qt.wigner(state, xvec, pvec)  # Uses optimal algorithm\n```\n\n## Saving and Data Format\n\n### CSV Storage Format\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\n\n# Create DataFrame with metadata\n# W is shape (1000, 1000) - rows are p values, columns are x values\n\n# Option 1: Save raw Wigner values\ndf = pd.DataFrame(W, index=pvec, columns=xvec)\ndf.to_csv('wigner.csv')\n\n# Option 2: Save with header information\n# First rows: metadata\n# Then: Wigner function values\n```\n\n## Dicke Model Context\nFor the Dicke model, the Wigner function of the cavity field (after tracing out spins) reveals:\n- Steady-state phase space structure\n- Effects of different dissipation channels\n- Coherence properties of the cavity\n- Phase transitions in open systems\n\n## References\n- QuTiP wigner function: http://qutip.org/docs/4.1.2/apidoc/functions.html#wigner\n- Wigner function theory: W. Vogel, D. Welsch \"Quantum Optics\" (3rd ed.)\n- Dicke model references: 1608.06293, 1611.03342 (arxiv.org)\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":943,"cache_read_input_tokens":19456,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":943,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":24},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e0305cd3-9616-4b8b-9c56-e058f1374831","uuid":"fd0d3a68-1174-42b6-840a-3a6885f46f8b"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_017kpPwfuxXxU6VACNHk5a2D","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01TzWpUMTCZigvuag8bJaYzW","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/environment/skills/dicke-model.md","content":"# Open Dicke Model - Quantum Optics Collective Coupling\n\n## Overview\nThe Dicke model describes N identical two-level systems (qubits/atoms) collectively coupled to a single cavity mode. It exhibits rich physics including superradiance and quantum phase transitions.\n\n## Hamiltonian\n```\nH = ω₀J_z + ω_c a†a + g(a† + a)(J₊ + J₋)\n```\n\nWhere:\n- `ω₀`: Transition frequency of two-level systems\n- `ω_c`: Cavity mode frequency\n- `a, a†`: Annihilation/creation operators for cavity\n- `J_z, J_±`: Collective angular momentum operators\n- `g`: Light-matter coupling strength\n- `J₊ = Σᵢ σ₊ⁱ`: Collective raising operator\n- `J₋ = Σᵢ σ₋ⁱ`: Collective lowering operator\n\n## Collective Operators in QuTiP\n\n### Building Collective Operators\n```python\nimport qutip as qt\nimport numpy as np\n\nN = 4  # Number of qubits\n\n# Single qubit operators\nsx = qt.sigmax()\nsy = qt.sigmay()\nsz = qt.sigmaz()\nsp = qt.sigmap()  # σ₊\nsm = qt.sigmam()  # σ₋\n\n# Build collective operators as sum over all N qubits\n# This requires tensor product construction\n```\n\n### Tensor Product Construction for N Qubits\n```python\ndef build_collective_op(single_op, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build collective operator for N qubits.\n    Example: Build Σᵢ σ_z^i\n    \"\"\"\n    J = None\n    for i in range(N):\n        # Tensor product: I⊗I⊗...⊗O⊗...⊗I\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = single_op\n        term = qt.tensor(*op_list)\n\n        if J is None:\n            J = term\n        else:\n            J += term\n    return J\n\n# Collective operators\nJz = build_collective_op(sz, N)\nJp = build_collective_op(sp, N)  # J₊\nJm = build_collective_op(sm, N)  # J₋\n```\n\n## Hamiltonian Construction for Dicke Model\n\n```python\n# System parameters\nN = 4\nomega0 = 1.0      # Two-level frequency\nomega_c = 1.0     # Cavity frequency\ng = 2.0 / np.sqrt(N)  # Coupling strength\nkappa = 1.0       # Cavity decay rate\nn_max = 16        # Photon number cutoff\n\n# Cavity operators\na = qt.destroy(n_max)\na_dag = qt.create(n_max)\n\n# Collective operators for N qubits\nJz = build_collective_op(sz, N)\nJp = build_collective_op(sp, N)\nJm = build_collective_op(sm, N)\n\n# Full Hamiltonian (cavity + qubits + interaction)\nH_cavity = omega_c * qt.tensor(a_dag * a, qt.qeye(2**N))\nH_qubits = omega0 * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jz)\nH_interaction = g * qt.tensor(a + a_dag, Jp + Jm)\n\nH = H_cavity + H_qubits + H_interaction\n```\n\n## Open System: Collapse Operators\n\n### Local Dephasing\n```python\n# γ_φ: Local dephasing rate\n# Operator: σ_z for each qubit\ngamma_phi = 0.01\n\n# Dephasing collapse operators\nc_dephasing = []\nfor i in range(N):\n    op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n    op_list[i] = sz\n    c_dephasing.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list)))\n```\n\n### Local Pumping (Excitation)\n```python\n# γ_↑: Local pumping rate\n# Operator: σ₊ for each qubit\ngamma_up = 0.1\n\n# Pumping collapse operators\nc_pumping = []\nfor i in range(N):\n    op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n    op_list[i] = sp\n    c_pumping.append(np.sqrt(gamma_up) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list)))\n```\n\n### Local Emission (De-excitation)\n```python\n# γ_↓: Local emission rate\n# Operator: σ₋ for each qubit\ngamma_down = 0.1\n\n# Emission collapse operators\nc_emission = []\nfor i in range(N):\n    op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n    op_list[i] = sm\n    c_emission.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list)))\n```\n\n### Collective Pumping\n```python\n# γ_⇑: Collective pumping rate\n# Operator: J₊ (sum of all σ₊)\ngamma_col_up = 0.1\n\nJp_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jp)\nc_col_pumping = [np.sqrt(gamma_col_up) * Jp_full]\n```\n\n### Collective Emission\n```python\n# γ_⇓: Collective emission rate\n# Operator: J₋ (sum of all σ₋)\ngamma_col_down = 0.1\n\nJm_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jm)\nc_col_emission = [np.sqrt(gamma_col_down) * Jm_full]\n```\n\n### Cavity Loss\n```python\n# κ: Cavity decay rate\n# Operator: a (annihilation operator)\nkappa = 1.0\n\na_full = qt.tensor(a, qt.qeye(2**N))\nc_cavity = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a_full]\n```\n\n## Four Loss Cases Implementation\n\n### Case 1: Local dephasing + Local pumping\n```python\nc_ops = c_dephasing + c_pumping + [np.sqrt(kappa) * a_full]\n```\n\n### Case 2: Local dephasing + Local emission\n```python\nc_ops = c_dephasing + c_emission + [np.sqrt(kappa) * a_full]\n```\n\n### Case 3: Local dephasing + Local emission + Collective pumping\n```python\nc_ops = c_dephasing + c_emission + c_col_pumping + [np.sqrt(kappa) * a_full]\n```\n\n### Case 4: Local dephasing + Local emission + Collective emission\n```python\nc_ops = c_dephasing + c_emission + c_col_emission + [np.sqrt(kappa) * a_full]\n```\n\n## Steady State Calculation\n\n```python\n# Find steady state\nrho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops)\n\n# Alternatively, with options\nopts = qt.Options(nsteps=10000, rtol=1e-8, atol=1e-10)\nrho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, options=opts)\n```\n\n## Extracting Cavity State\n\n```python\n# After finding steady state rho_ss\n# Trace out N qubits, keep cavity mode\n\n# Partition: first dimension is cavity (n_max), rest are qubits (2^N)\nmask = [True] + [False] * N  # Keep cavity (index 0)\nrho_cavity = qt.ptrace(rho_ss, mask)\n\n# Now rho_cavity is a state in the cavity Hilbert space (dimension n_max)\n```\n\n## Physical Interpretation\n- Local dephasing breaks coherence between qubits\n- Local pumping excites individual qubits\n- Local emission de-excites individual qubits\n- Collective processes break down super-radiance\n- These mechanisms model realistic dissipation in experimental systems\n\n## References\n- C. Emary and T. Brandes, \"Chaos and the quantum phase transition in the Dicke model,\" PRA 63, 053604 (2001)\n- K. Baumann et al., \"Dicke quantum phase transition in a superfluid gas,\" Nature 464, 1301 (2010)\n- Papers: 1608.06293, 1611.03342 (arxiv.org)\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1243,"cache_read_input_tokens":20399,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1243,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e0305cd3-9616-4b8b-9c56-e058f1374831","uuid":"8624f9d4-2be6-46c6-922a-5c8fadc5e372"}

It exhibits rich physics including superradiance and quantum phase transitions.\n\n## Hamiltonian\n```\nH = ω₀J_z + ω_c a†a + g(a† + a)(J₊ + J₋)\n```\n\nWhere:\n- `ω₀`: Transition frequency of two-level systems\n- `ω_c`: Cavity mode frequency\n- `a, a†`: Annihilation/creation operators for cavity\n- `J_z, J_±`: Collective angular momentum operators\n- `g`: Light-matter coupling strength\n- `J₊ = Σᵢ σ₊ⁱ`: Collective raising operator\n- `J₋ = Σᵢ σ₋ⁱ`: Collective lowering operator\n\n## Collective Operators in QuTiP\n\n### Building Collective Operators\n```python\nimport qutip as qt\nimport numpy as np\n\nN = 4  # Number of qubits\n\n# Single qubit operators\nsx = qt.sigmax()\nsy = qt.sigmay()\nsz = qt.sigmaz()\nsp = qt.sigmap()  # σ₊\nsm = qt.sigmam()  # σ₋\n\n# Build collective operators as sum over all N qubits\n# This requires tensor product construction\n```\n\n### Tensor Product Construction for N Qubits\n```python\ndef build_collective_op(single_op, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build collective operator for N qubits.\n    Example: Build Σᵢ σ_z^i\n    \"\"\"\n    J = None\n    for i in range(N):\n        # Tensor product: I⊗I⊗...⊗O⊗...⊗I\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = single_op\n        term = qt.tensor(*op_list)\n\n        if J is None:\n            J = term\n        else:\n            J += term\n    return J\n\n# Collective operators\nJz = build_collective_op(sz, N)\nJp = build_collective_op(sp, N)  # J₊\nJm = build_collective_op(sm, N)  # J₋\n```\n\n## Hamiltonian Construction for Dicke Model\n\n```python\n# System parameters\nN = 4\nomega0 = 1.0      # Two-level frequency\nomega_c = 1.0     # Cavity frequency\ng = 2.0 / np.sqrt(N)  # Coupling strength\nkappa = 1.0       # Cavity decay rate\nn_max = 16        # Photon number cutoff\n\n# Cavity operators\na = qt.destroy(n_max)\na_dag = qt.create(n_max)\n\n# Collective operators for N qubits\nJz = build_collective_op(sz, N)\nJp = build_collective_op(sp, N)\nJm = build_collective_op(sm, N)\n\n# Full Hamiltonian (cavity + qubits + interaction)\nH_cavity = omega_c * qt.tensor(a_dag * a, qt.qeye(2**N))\nH_qubits = omega0 * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jz)\nH_interaction = g * qt.tensor(a + a_dag, Jp + Jm)\n\nH = H_cavity + H_qubits + H_interaction\n```\n\n## Open System: Collapse Operators\n\n### Local Dephasing\n```python\n# γ_φ: Local dephasing rate\n# Operator: σ_z for each qubit\ngamma_phi = 0.01\n\n# Dephasing collapse operators\nc_dephasing = []\nfor i in range(N):\n    op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n    op_list[i] = sz\n    c_dephasing.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list)))\n```\n\n### Local Pumping (Excitation)\n```python\n# γ_↑: Local pumping rate\n# Operator: σ₊ for each qubit\ngamma_up = 0.1\n\n# Pumping collapse operators\nc_pumping = []\nfor i in range(N):\n    op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n    op_list[i] = sp\n    c_pumping.append(np.sqrt(gamma_up) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list)))\n```\n\n### Local Emission (De-excitation)\n```python\n# γ_↓: Local emission rate\n# Operator: σ₋ for each qubit\ngamma_down = 0.1\n\n# Emission collapse operators\nc_emission = []\nfor i in range(N):\n    op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n    op_list[i] = sm\n    c_emission.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list)))\n```\n\n### Collective Pumping\n```python\n# γ_⇑: Collective pumping rate\n# Operator: J₊ (sum of all σ₊)\ngamma_col_up = 0.1\n\nJp_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jp)\nc_col_pumping = [np.sqrt(gamma_col_up) * Jp_full]\n```\n\n### Collective Emission\n```python\n# γ_⇓: Collective emission rate\n# Operator: J₋ (sum of all σ₋)\ngamma_col_down = 0.1\n\nJm_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jm)\nc_col_emission = [np.sqrt(gamma_col_down) * Jm_full]\n```\n\n### Cavity Loss\n```python\n# κ: Cavity decay rate\n# Operator: a (annihilation operator)\nkappa = 1.0\n\na_full = qt.tensor(a, qt.qeye(2**N))\nc_cavity = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a_full]\n```\n\n## Four Loss Cases Implementation\n\n### Case 1: Local dephasing + Local pumping\n```python\nc_ops = c_dephasing + c_pumping + [np.sqrt(kappa) * a_full]\n```\n\n### Case 2: Local dephasing + Local emission\n```python\nc_ops = c_dephasing + c_emission + [np.sqrt(kappa) * a_full]\n```\n\n### Case 3: Local dephasing + Local emission + Collective pumping\n```python\nc_ops = c_dephasing + c_emission + c_col_pumping + [np.sqrt(kappa) * a_full]\n```\n\n### Case 4: Local dephasing + Local emission + Collective emission\n```python\nc_ops = c_dephasing + c_emission + c_col_emission + [np.sqrt(kappa) * a_full]\n```\n\n## Steady State Calculation\n\n```python\n# Find steady state\nrho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops)\n\n# Alternatively, with options\nopts = qt.Options(nsteps=10000, rtol=1e-8, atol=1e-10)\nrho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, options=opts)\n```\n\n## Extracting Cavity State\n\n```python\n# After finding steady state rho_ss\n# Trace out N qubits, keep cavity mode\n\n# Partition: first dimension is cavity (n_max), rest are qubits (2^N)\nmask = [True] + [False] * N  # Keep cavity (index 0)\nrho_cavity = qt.ptrace(rho_ss, mask)\n\n# Now rho_cavity is a state in the cavity Hilbert space (dimension n_max)\n```\n\n## Physical Interpretation\n- Local dephasing breaks coherence between qubits\n- Local pumping excites individual qubits\n- Local emission de-excites individual qubits\n- Collective Number of two-level systems\nomega0 = 1.0  # Two-level system frequency\nomega_c = 1.0  # Cavity mode frequency\ng = 2.0 / np.sqrt(N)  # Coupling strength\nkappa = 1.0  # Cavity decay rate\nn_max = 16  # Photon number cutoff\n\n# Dissipation parameters\ngamma_phi = 0.01  # Local dephasing rate\ngamma_up = 0.1  # Local pumping rate\ngamma_down = 0.1  # Local emission rate\ngamma_col_up = 0.1  # Collective pumping rate\ngamma_col_down = 0.1  # Collective emission rate\n\n# Wigner function grid\nx_min, x_max = -6, 6\np_min, p_max = -6, 6\ngrid_points = 1000\n\nprint(f\"Open Dicke Model Simulation\")\nprint(f\"=\" * 60)\nprint(f\"Parameters:\")\nprint(f\"  N = {N} (number of qubits)\")\nprint(f\"  ω₀ = {omega0}, ωc = {omega_c}\")\nprint(f\"  g = {g:.6f}\")\nprint(f\"  κ = {kappa}, nmax = {n_max}\")\nprint(f\"  Grid: {grid_points}×{grid_points} from [{x_min},{x_max}]×[{p_min},{p_max}]\")\nprint()\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef build_collective_op(single_op, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build collective operator: sum over all N qubits\n    E.g., J_z = Σᵢ σ_z^i\n    \"\"\"\n    J = None\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = single_op\n        term = qt.tensor(*op_list)\n\n        if J is None:\n            J = term\n        else:\n            J += term\n    return J\n\n\ndef build_hamiltonian():\n    \"\"\"Build the Dicke model Hamiltonian\"\"\"\n    # Cavity operators\n    a = qt.destroy(n_max)\n    a_dag = qt.create(n_max)\n\n    # Single qubit operators\n    sz = qt.sigmaz()\n    sp = qt.sigmap()\n    sm = qt.sigmam()\n\n    # Collective operators\n    Jz = build_collective_op(sz, N)\n    Jp = build_collective_op(sp, N)  # J_+\n    Jm = build_collective_op(sm, N)  # J_-\n\n    # Hamiltonian terms\n    # H = ω₀ J_z + ωc a†a + g(a† + a)(J₊ + J₋)\n    H_qubits = omega0 * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jz)\n    H_cavity = omega_c * qt.tensor(a_dag * a, qt.qeye(2**N))\n    H_interaction = g * qt.tensor(a + a_dag, Jp + Jm)\n\n    H = H_qubits + H_cavity + H_interaction\n\n    return H, a, sz, sp, sm, Jz, Jp, Jm\n\n\ndef build_collapse_operators(a, sz, sp, sm, Jz, Jp, Jm):\n    \"\"\"Build collapse operators for different dissipation channels\"\"\"\n\n    # Cavity loss\n    a_full = qt.tensor(a, qt.qeye(2**N))\n    c_cavity = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a_full]\n\n    # Local dephasing (all qubits)\n    c_dephasing = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sz\n        c_dephasing.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Local pumping (all qubits)\n    c_pumping = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sp\n        c_pumping.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_up) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Local emission (all qubits)\n    c_emission = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sm\n        c_emission.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_down) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Collective pumping\n    Jp_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jp)\n    c_col_pumping = [np.sqrt(gamma_col_up) * Jp_full]\n\n    # Collective emission\n    Jm_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jm)\n    c_col_emission = [np.sqrt(gamma_col_down) * Jm_full]\n\n    return {\n        \"cavity\": c_cavity,\n        \"dephasing\": c_dephasing,\n        \"pumping\": c_pumping,\n        \"emission\": c_emission,\n        \"col_pumping\": c_col_pumping,\n        \"col_emission\": c_col_emission,\n    }\n\n\ndef solve_steady_state(H, c_ops, case_name=\"\"):\n    \"\"\"Solve for steady state density matrix\"\"\"\n    print(f\"Solving steady state {case_name}...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    try:\n        # Set solver options for better convergence\n        opts = qt.Options(nsteps=10000, rtol=1e-8, atol=1e-10)\n        rho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, options=opts)\n        print(\"Done.\")\n        return rho_ss\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n\ndef extract_cavity_state(rho_ss):\n    \"\"\"\n    Extract cavity state by tracing out qubits\n    Returns reduced density matrix for cavity only\n    \"\"\"\n    # Partition: cavity (dimension n_max) + N qubits (dimension 2^N)\n    # Keep only cavity (index 0)\n    mask = [True] + [False] * N\n    rho_cavity = qt.ptrace(rho_ss, mask)\n    return rho_cavity\n\n\ndef calculate_wigner_function(rho_cavity):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Wigner function on grid\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Calculating Wigner function on {grid_points}×{grid_points} grid...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    xvec = np.linspace(x_min, x_max, grid_points)\n    pvec = np.linspace(p_min, p_max, grid_points)\n\n    W = qt.wigner(rho_cavity, xvec, pvec)\n\n    print(\"Done.\")\n    return W, xvec, pvec\n\n\ndef save_wigner_csv(W, xvec, pvec, filename):\n    \"\"\"Save Wigner function to CSV file\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Saving to {filename}...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    # Create DataFrame with p as index and x as columns\n    df = pd.DataFrame(W, index=pvec, columns=xvec)\n    df.index.name = \"p\"\n    df.columns.name = \"x\"\n\n    df.to_csv(filename, float_format=\"%.10e\")\n    print(\"Done.\")\n\n\ndef analyze_state(rho_cavity, case_num):\n    \"\"\"Analyze properties of the cavity state\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Analysis for case {case_num}:\")\n\n    # Photon number expectation value\n    a_op = qt.destroy(n_max)\n    n_expect = qt.expect(a_op.dag() * a_op, rho_cavity)\n    print(f\"    <n> = {n_expect:.6f}\")\n\n    # Purity\n    purity = qt.purity(rho_cavity)\n    print(f\"    Purity = {purity:.6f}\")\n\n    # Von Neumann entropy\n    entropy = qt.entropy_vn(rho_cavity)\n    print(f\"    Entropy = {entropy:.6f}\")\n\n    return {\"photon_number\": n_expect, \"purity\": purity, \"entropy\": entropy}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Simulation\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef main():\n    # Ensure output directory exists\n    os.makedirs(\".\", exist_ok=True)\n\n    # Build Hamiltonian\n    print(\"\\nBuilding Hamiltonian...\")\n    H, a, sz, sp, sm, Jz, Jp, Jm = build_hamiltonian()\n    print(f\"  Hilbert space dimension: {H.dims[0]}\")\n\n    # Build collapse operators\n    print(\"\\nBuilding collapse operators...\")\n    c_ops_dict = build_collapse_operators(a, sz, sp, sm, Jz, Jp, Jm)\n\n    # Define the 4 cases\n    cases = [\n        {\n            \"name\": \"Local dephasing + Local pumping\",\n            \"c_ops\": (\n                c_ops_dict[\"cavity\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"dephasing\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"pumping\"]\n            ),\n            \"file\": \"1.csv\",\n        },\n        {\n            \"name\": \"Local dephasing + Local emission\",\n            \"c_ops\": (\n                c_ops_dict[\"cavity\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"dephasing\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"emission\"]\n            ),\n            \"file\": \"2.csv\",\n        },\n        {\n            \"name\": \"Local dephasing + Local emission + Collective pumping\",\n            \"c_ops\": (\n                c_ops_dict[\"cavity\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"dephasing\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"emission\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"col_pumping\"]\n            ),\n            \"file\": \"3.csv\",\n        },\n        {\n            \"name\": \"Local dephasing + Local emission + Collective emission\",\n            \"c_ops\": (\n                c_ops_dict[\"cavity\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"dephasing\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"emission\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"col_emission\"]\n            ),\n            \"file\": \"4.csv\",\n        },\n    ]\n\n    # Solve steady state\n        rho_ss = solve_steady_state(H, case[\"c_ops\"], f\"for case {case_num}\")\n        if rho_ss is None:\n            print(f\"ERROR: Failed to solve case {case_num}\")\n            continue\n\n        # Extract cavity state\n        print(f\"Extracting cavity state...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n        rho_cavity = extract_cavity_state(rho_ss)\n        print(\"Done.\")\n\n        # Analyze state properties\n        analysis = analyze_state(rho_cavity, case_num)\n        analysis[\"case\"] = case_num\n        analysis[\"name\"] = case[\"name\"]\n        analysis_results.append(analysis)\n\n        # Save to CSV\n        save_wigner_csv(W, xvec, pvec, case[\"file\"])\n\n    # Print summary\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"Summary of Results\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    for result in analysis_results:\n        print(f\"\\nCase {result['case']}: {result['name']}\")\n        print(f\"  <n> = {result['photon_number']:.6f}\")\n        print(f\"  Purity = {result['purity']:.6f}\")\n        print(f\"  Entropy = {result['entropy']:.6f}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nWigner functions saved to:\")\n    for i, case in enumerate(cases, 1):\n        print(f\"  Case {i}: {case['file']}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nGrid information:\")\n    print(f\"  x-axis: [{x_min}, {x_max}] with {grid_points} points\")\n    print(f\"  p-axis: [{p_min}, {p_max}] with {grid_points} points\")\n    print(f\"  Total data points per file: {grid_points}×{grid_points}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":260,"cache_read_input_tokens":25643,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":260,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e0305cd3-9616-4b8b-9c56-e058f1374831","uuid":"f3f652db-7a11-4c94-954b-10b55bdfeb2c"}

Solve steady state\n        rho_ss = solve_steady_state(H, case[\"c_ops\"], f\"for case {case_num}\")\n        if rho_ss is None:\n            print(f\"ERROR: Failed to solve case {case_num}\")\n            continue\n\n        # Extract cavity state\n        print(f\"Extracting cavity state...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n        rho_cavity = extract_cavity_state(rho_ss)\n        print(\"Done.\")\n\n        # Analyze state properties\n        analysis = analyze_state(rho_cavity, case_num)\n        analysis[\"case\"] = case_num\n        analysis[\"name\"] = case[\"name\"]\n        analysis_results.append(analysis)\n\n        # Calculate Wigner function\n        W, xvec, pvec = calculate_wigner_function(rho_cavity)\n\n        # Verify normalization\n        integral = np.trapz(np.trapz(W, pvec, axis=0), xvec)\n        print(f\"  Wigner function integral (normalization check): {integral:.10f}\")\n\n        # Save to CSV\n        save_wigner_csv(W, xvec, pvec, case[\"file\"])\n\n    # Print summary\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"Summary of Results\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    for result in analysis_results:\n        print(f\"\\nCase {result['case']}: {result['name']}\")\n        print(f\"  <n> = {result['photon_number']:.6f}\")\n        print(f\"  Purity = {result['purity']:.6f}\")\n        print(f\"  Entropy = {result['entropy']:.6f}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nWigner functions saved to:\")\n    for i, case in enumerate(cases, 1):\n        print(f\"  Case {i}: {case['file']}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nGrid information:\")\n    print(f\"  x-axis: [{x_min}, {x_max}] with {grid_points} points\")\n    print(f\"  p-axis: [{p_min}, {p_max}] with {grid_points} points\")\n    print(f\"  Total data points per file: {grid_points}×{grid_points}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Number of two-level systems\nomega0 = 1.0  # Two-level system frequency\nomega_c = 1.0  # Cavity mode frequency\ng = 2.0 / np.sqrt(N)  # Coupling strength\nkappa = 1.0  # Cavity decay rate\nn_max = 16  # Photon number cutoff\n\n# Dissipation parameters\ngamma_phi = 0.01  # Local dephasing rate\ngamma_up = 0.1  # Local pumping rate\ngamma_down = 0.1  # Local emission rate\ngamma_col_up = 0.1  # Collective pumping rate\ngamma_col_down = 0.1  # Collective emission rate\n\n# Wigner function grid\nx_min, x_max = -6, 6\np_min, p_max = -6, 6\ngrid_points = 1000\n\nprint(f\"Open Dicke Model Simulation\")\nprint(f\"=\" * 60)\nprint(f\"Parameters:\")\nprint(f\"  N = {N} (number of qubits)\")\nprint(f\"  ω₀ = {omega0}, ωc = {omega_c}\")\nprint(f\"  g = {g:.6f}\")\nprint(f\"  κ = {kappa}, nmax = {n_max}\")\nprint(f\"  Grid: {grid_points}×{grid_points} from [{x_min},{x_max}]×[{p_min},{p_max}]\")\nprint()\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef build_collective_op(single_op, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build collective operator: sum over all N qubits\n    E.g., J_z = Σᵢ σ_z^i\n    \"\"\"\n    J = None\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = single_op\n        term = qt.tensor(*op_list)\n\n        if J is None:\n            J = term\n        else:\n            J += term\n    return J\n\n\ndef build_hamiltonian():\n    \"\"\"Build the Dicke model Hamiltonian\"\"\"\n    # Cavity operators\n    a = qt.destroy(n_max)\n    a_dag = qt.create(n_max)\n\n    # Single qubit operators\n    sz = qt.sigmaz()\n    sp = qt.sigmap()\n    sm = qt.sigmam()\n\n    # Collective operators\n    Jz = build_collective_op(sz, N)\n    Jp = build_collective_op(sp, N)  # J_+\n    Jm = build_collective_op(sm, N)  # J_-\n\n    # Hamiltonian terms\n    # H = ω₀ J_z + ωc a†a + g(a† + a)(J₊ + J₋)\n    H_qubits = omega0 * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jz)\n    H_cavity = omega_c * qt.tensor(a_dag * a, qt.qeye(2**N))\n    H_interaction = g * qt.tensor(a + a_dag, Jp + Jm)\n\n    H = H_qubits + H_cavity + H_interaction\n\n    return H, a, sz, sp, sm, Jz, Jp, Jm\n\n\ndef build_collapse_operators(a, sz, sp, sm, Jz, Jp, Jm):\n    \"\"\"Build collapse operators for different dissipation channels\"\"\"\n\n    # Cavity loss\n    a_full = qt.tensor(a, qt.qeye(2**N))\n    c_cavity = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a_full]\n\n    # Local dephasing (all qubits)\n    c_dephasing = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sz\n        c_dephasing.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Local pumping (all qubits)\n    c_pumping = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sp\n        c_pumping.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_up) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Local emission (all qubits)\n    c_emission = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sm\n        c_emission.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_down) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Collective pumping\n    Jp_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jp)\n    c_col_pumping = [np.sqrt(gamma_col_up) * Jp_full]\n\n    # Collective emission\n    Jm_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jm)\n    c_col_emission = [np.sqrt(gamma_col_down) * Jm_full]\n\n    return {\n        \"cavity\": c_cavity,\n        \"dephasing\": c_dephasing,\n        \"pumping\": c_pumping,\n        \"emission\": c_emission,\n        \"col_pumping\": c_col_pumping,\n        \"col_emission\": c_col_emission,\n    }\n\n\ndef solve_steady_state(H, c_ops, case_name=\"\"):\n    \"\"\"Solve for steady state density matrix\"\"\"\n    print(f\"Solving steady state {case_name}...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    try:\n        # Set solver options for better convergence\n        opts = qt.Options(nsteps=10000, rtol=1e-8, atol=1e-10)\n        rho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, options=opts)\n        print(\"Done.\")\n        return rho_ss\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n\ndef extract_cavity_state(rho_ss):\n    \"\"\"\n    Extract cavity state by tracing out qubits\n    Returns reduced density matrix for cavity only\n    \"\"\"\n    # Partition: cavity (dimension n_max) + N qubits (dimension 2^N)\n    # Keep only cavity (index 0)\n    mask = [True] + [False] * N\n    rho_cavity = qt.ptrace(rho_ss, mask)\n    return rho_cavity\n\n\ndef calculate_wigner_function(rho_cavity):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Wigner function on grid\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Calculating Wigner function on {grid_points}×{grid_points} grid...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    xvec = np.linspace(x_min, x_max, grid_points)\n    pvec = np.linspace(p_min, p_max, grid_points)\n\n    W = qt.wigner(rho_cavity, xvec, pvec)\n\n    print(\"Done.\")\n    return W, xvec, pvec\n\n\ndef save_wigner_csv(W, xvec, pvec, filename):\n    \"\"\"Save Wigner function to CSV file\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Saving to {filename}...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    # Create DataFrame with p as index and x as columns\n    df = pd.DataFrame(W, index=pvec, columns=xvec)\n    df.index.name = \"p\"\n    df.columns.name = \"x\"\n\n    df.to_csv(filename, float_format=\"%.10e\")\n    print(\"Done.\")\n\n\ndef analyze_state(rho_cavity, case_num):\n    \"\"\"Analyze properties of the cavity state\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Analysis for case {case_num}:\")\n\n    # Photon number expectation value\n    a_op = qt.destroy(n_max)\n    n_expect = qt.expect(a_op.dag() * a_op, rho_cavity)\n    print(f\"    <n> = {n_expect:.6f}\")\n\n    # Purity\n    purity = qt.purity(rho_cavity)\n    print(f\"    Purity = {purity:.6f}\")\n\n    # Von Neumann entropy\n    entropy = qt.entropy_vn(rho_cavity)\n    print(f\"    Entropy = {entropy:.6f}\")\n\n    return {\"photon_number\": n_expect, \"purity\": purity, \"entropy\": entropy}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Simulation\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef main():\n    # Number of two-level systems\nomega0 = 1.0  # Two-level system frequency\nomega_c = 1.0  # Cavity mode frequency\ng = 2.0 / np.sqrt(N)  # Coupling strength\nkappa = 1.0  # Cavity decay rate\nn_max = 16  # Cavity operators\n    a = qt.destroy(n_max)\n    a_dag = qt.create(n_max)\n\n    # Single qubit operators\n    sz = qt.sigmaz()\n    sp = qt.sigmap()\n    sm = qt.sigmam()\n\n    # Collective operators for N qubits\n    Jz = build_collective_op(sz, N)\n    Jp = build_collective_op(sp, N)  # J_+\n    Jm = build_collective_op(sm, N)  # J_-\n\n    # Full-system operators (cavity tensor qubit_system)\n    a_full = qt.tensor(a, qt.qeye(2**N))\n    Jz_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jz)\n    Jp_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jp)\n    Jm_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jm)\n\n    # Hamiltonian terms\n    # H = ω₀ J_z + ωc a†a + g(a† + a)(J₊ + J₋)\n    H_qubits = omega0 * Jz_full\n    H_cavity = omega_c * a_full.dag() * a_full\n    H_interaction = g * (a_full + a_full.dag()) * (Jp_full + Jm_full)\n\n    H = H_qubits + H_cavity + H_interaction\n\n    return H, a, sz, sp, sm, Jz, Jp, Jm\n\n\ndef build_collapse_operators(a, sz, sp, sm, Jz, Jp, Jm):\n    \"\"\"Build collapse operators for different dissipation channels\"\"\"\n\n    # Cavity loss\n    a_full = qt.tensor(a, qt.qeye(2**N))\n    c_cavity = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a_full]\n\n    # Local dephasing (all qubits)\n    c_dephasing = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sz\n        c_dephasing.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Local pumping (all qubits)\n    c_pumping = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sp\n        c_pumping.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_up) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Local emission (all qubits)\n    c_emission = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sm\n        c_emission.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_down) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Collective pumping\n    Jp_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jp)\n    c_col_pumping = [np.sqrt(gamma_col_up) * Jp_full]\n\n    # Collective emission\n    Jm_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jm)\n    c_col_emission = [np.sqrt(gamma_col_down) * Jm_full]\n\n    return {\n        \"cavity\": c_cavity,\n        \"dephasing\": c_dephasing,\n        \"pumping\": c_pumping,\n        \"emission\": c_emission,\n        \"col_pumping\": c_col_pumping,\n        \"col_emission\": c_col_emission,\n    }\n\n\ndef solve_steady_state(H, c_ops, case_name=\"\"):\n    \"\"\"Solve for steady state density matrix\"\"\"\n    print(f\"Solving steady state {case_name}...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    try:\n        # Set solver options for better convergence\n        opts = qt.Options(nsteps=10000, rtol=1e-8, atol=1e-10)\n        rho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, options=opts)\n        print(\"Done.\")\n        return rho_ss\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n\ndef extract_cavity_state(rho_ss):\n    \"\"\"\n    Extract cavity state by tracing out qubits\n    Returns reduced density matrix for cavity only\n    \"\"\"\n    # Partition: cavity (dimension n_max) + N qubits (dimension 2^N)\n    # Keep only cavity (index 0)\n    mask = [True] + [False] * N\n    rho_cavity = qt.ptrace(rho_ss, mask)\n    return rho_cavity\n\n\ndef calculate_wigner_function(rho_cavity):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Wigner function on grid\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Calculating Wigner function on {grid_points}×{grid_points} grid...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    xvec = np.linspace(x_min, x_max, grid_points)\n    pvec = np.linspace(p_min, p_max, grid_points)\n\n    W = qt.wigner(rho_cavity, xvec, pvec)\n\n    print(\"Done.\")\n    return W, xvec, pvec\n\n\ndef save_wigner_csv(W, xvec, pvec, filename):\n    \"\"\"Save Wigner function to CSV file\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Saving to {filename}...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    # Create DataFrame with p as index and x as columns\n    df = pd.DataFrame(W, index=pvec, columns=xvec)\n    df.index.name = \"p\"\n    df.columns.name = \"x\"\n\n    df.to_csv(filename, float_format=\"%.10e\")\n    print(\"Done.\")\n\n\ndef analyze_state(rho_cavity, case_num):\n    \"\"\"Analyze properties of the cavity state\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Analysis for case {case_num}:\")\n\n    # Photon number expectation value\n    a_op = qt.destroy(n_max)\n    n_expect = qt.expect(a_op.dag() * a_op, rho_cavity)\n    print(f\"    <n> = {n_expect:.6f}\")\n\n    # Purity\n    purity = qt.purity(rho_cavity)\n    print(f\"    Purity = {purity:.6f}\")\n\n    # Von Neumann entropy\n    entropy = qt.entropy_vn(rho_cavity)\n    print(f\"    Entropy = {entropy:.6f}\")\n\n    return {\"photon_number\": n_expect, \"purity\": purity, \"entropy\": entropy}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Simulation\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef main():\n    # Ensure output directory exists\n    os.makedirs(\".\", exist_ok=True)\n\n    # Build Hamiltonian\n    print(\"\\nBuilding Hamiltonian...\")\n    H, a, sz, sp, sm, Jz, Jp, Jm = build_hamiltonian()\n    print(f\"  Hilbert space dimension: {H.dims[0]}\")\n\n    # Build collapse operators\n    print(\"\\nBuilding collapse operators...\")\n    c_ops_dict = build_collapse_operators(a, sz, sp, sm, Jz, Jp, Jm)\n\n    # Define the 4 cases\n    cases = [\n        {\n            \"name\": \"Local dephasing + Local pumping\",\n            \"c_ops\": (\n                c_ops_dict[\"cavity\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"dephasing\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"pumping\"]\n            ),\n            \"file\": \"1.csv\",\n        },\n        {\n            \"name\": \"Local dephasing + Local emission\",\n            \"c_ops\": (\n                c_ops_dict[\"cavity\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"dephasing\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"emission\"]\n            ),\n            \"file\": \"2.csv\",\n        },\n        {\n            \"name\": \"Local dephasing + Local emission + Collective pumping\",\n            \"c_ops\": (\n                c_ops_dict[\"cavity\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"dephasing\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"emission\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"col_pumping\"]\n            ),\n            \"file\": \"3.csv\",\n        },\n        {\n            \"name\": \"Local dephasing + Local emission + Collective emission\",\n            \"c_ops\": (\n                c_ops_dict[\"cavity\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"dephasing\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"emission\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"col_emission\"]\n            ),\n            \"file\": \"4.csv\",\n        },\n    ]\n\n    # Store analysis results\n    analysis_results = []\n\n    # Process each case\n    for case_num, case in enumerate(cases, 1):\n        print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n        print(f\"Case {case_num}: {case['name']}\")\n        print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n\n        # Solve steady state\n        rho_ss = solve_steady_state(H, case[\"c_ops\"], f\"for case {case_num}\")\n        if rho_ss is None:\n            print(f\"ERROR: Failed to solve case {case_num}\")\n            continue\n\n        # Extract cavity state\n        print(f\"Extracting cavity state...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n        rho_cavity = extract_cavity_state(rho_ss)\n        print(\"Done.\")\n\n        # Analyze state properties\n        analysis = analyze_state(rho_cavity, case_num)\n        analysis[\"case\"] = case_num\n        analysis[\"name\"] = case[\"name\"]\n        analysis_results.append(analysis)\n\n        # Calculate Wigner function\n        W, xvec, pvec = calculate_wigner_function(rho_cavity)\n\n        # Verify normalization\n        integral = np.trapz(np.trapz(W, pvec, axis=0), xvec)\n        print(f\"  Wigner function integral (normalization check): {integral:.10f}\")\n\n        # Save to CSV\n        save_wigner_csv(W, xvec, pvec, case[\"file\"])\n\n    # Print summary\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"Summary of Results\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    for result in analysis_results:\n        print(f\"\\nCase {result['case']}: {result['name']}\")\n        print(f\"  <n> = {result['photon_number']:.6f}\")\n        print(f\"  Purity = {result['purity']:.6f}\")\n        print(f\"  Entropy = {result['entropy']:.6f}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nWigner functions saved to:\")\n    for i, case in enumerate(cases, 1):\n        print(f\"  Case {i}: {case['file']}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nGrid information:\")\n    print(f\"  x-axis: [{x_min}, {x_max}] with {grid_points} points\")\n    print(f\"  p-axis: [{p_min}, {p_max}] with {grid_points} points\")\n    print(f\"  Total data points per file: {grid_points}×{grid_points}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":77,"oldLines":7,"newStart":77,"newLines":7,"lines":["     sp = qt.sigmap()","     sm = qt.sigmam()"," ","-    # Number of two-level systems\nomega0 = 1.0  # Two-level system frequency\nomega_c = 1.0  # Cavity mode frequency\ng = 2.0 / np.sqrt(N)  # Coupling strength\nkappa = 1.0  # Cavity decay rate\nn_max = 16  # Cavity operators\n    a = qt.destroy(n_max)\n    a_dag = qt.create(n_max)\n\n    # Single qubit operators\n    sz = qt.sigmaz()\n    sp = qt.sigmap()\n    sm = qt.sigmam()\n\n    # Collective operators for N qubits (acting only in qubit space)\n    Jz = build_collective_op(sz, N)\n    Jp = build_collective_op(sp, N)  # J_+\n    Jm = build_collective_op(sm, N)  # J_-\n\n    # Full-system operators (cavity tensor qubit_system)\n    a_full = qt.tensor(a, qt.qeye(2**N))\n    Jz_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jz)\n    Jp_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jp)\n    Jm_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jm)\n\n    # Hamiltonian terms\n    # H = ω₀ J_z + ωc a†a + g(a† + a)(J₊ + J₋)\n    H_qubits = omega0 * Jz_full\n    H_cavity = omega_c * a_full.dag() * a_full\n    # Interaction: g(a† + a)(J₊ + J₋) = g*a*(Jp+Jm) + g*a†*(Jp+Jm)\n    H_interaction = g * a_full * (Jp_full + Jm_full) + g * a_full.dag() * (Jp_full + Jm_full)\n\n    H = H_qubits + H_cavity + H_interaction\n\n    return H, a, sz, sp, sm, Jz, Jp, Jm\n\n\ndef build_collapse_operators(a, sz, sp, sm, Jz, Jp, Jm):\n    \"\"\"Build collapse operators for different dissipation channels\"\"\"\n\n    # Cavity loss\n    a_full = qt.tensor(a, qt.qeye(2**N))\n    c_cavity = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a_full]\n\n    # Local dephasing (all qubits)\n    c_dephasing = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sz\n        c_dephasing.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Local emission (all qubits)\n    c_emission = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sm\n        c_emission.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_down) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Collective pumping\n    Jp_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jp)\n    c_col_pumping = [np.sqrt(gamma_col_up) * Jp_full]\n\n    # Collective emission\n    Jm_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jm)\n    c_col_emission = [np.sqrt(gamma_col_down) * Jm_full]\n\n    return {\n        \"cavity\": c_cavity,\n        \"dephasing\": c_dephasing,\n        \"pumping\": c_pumping,\n        \"emission\": c_emission,\n        \"col_pumping\": c_col_pumping,\n        \"col_emission\": c_col_emission,\n    }\n\n\ndef solve_steady_state(H, c_ops, case_name=\"\"):\n    \"\"\"Solve for steady state density matrix\"\"\"\n    print(f\"Solving steady state {case_name}...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    try:\n        # Set solver options for better convergence\n        opts = qt.Options(nsteps=10000, rtol=1e-8, atol=1e-10)\n        rho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, options=opts)\n        print(\"Done.\")\n        return rho_ss\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n\ndef extract_cavity_state(rho_ss):\n    \"\"\"\n    Extract cavity state by tracing out qubits\n    Returns reduced density matrix for cavity only\n    \"\"\"\n    # Partition: cavity (dimension n_max) + N qubits (dimension 2^N)\n    # Keep only cavity (index 0)\n    mask = [True] + [False] * N\n    rho_cavity = qt.ptrace(rho_ss, mask)\n    return rho_cavity\n\n\ndef calculate_wigner_function(rho_cavity):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Wigner function on grid\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Calculating Wigner function on {grid_points}×{grid_points} grid...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    xvec = np.linspace(x_min, x_max, grid_points)\n    pvec = np.linspace(p_min, p_max, grid_points)\n\n    W = qt.wigner(rho_cavity, xvec, pvec)\n\n    print(\"Done.\")\n    return W, xvec, pvec\n\n\ndef save_wigner_csv(W, xvec, pvec, filename):\n    \"\"\"Save Wigner function to CSV file\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Saving to {filename}...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    # Create DataFrame with p as index and x as columns\n    df = pd.DataFrame(W, index=pvec, columns=xvec)\n    df.index.name = \"p\"\n    df.columns.name = \"x\"\n\n    df.to_csv(filename, float_format=\"%.10e\")\n    print(\"Done.\")\n\n\ndef analyze_state(rho_cavity, case_num):\n    \"\"\"Analyze properties of the cavity state\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Analysis for case {case_num}:\")\n\n    # Photon number expectation value\n    a_op = qt.destroy(n_max)\n    n_expect = qt.expect(a_op.dag() * a_op, rho_cavity)\n    print(f\"    <n> = {n_expect:.6f}\")\n\n    # Purity\n    purity = qt.purity(rho_cavity)\n    print(f\"    Purity = {purity:.6f}\")\n\n    # Number of two-level systems\nomega0 = 1.0  # Two-level system frequency\nomega_c = 1.0  # Cavity mode frequency\ng = 2.0 / np.sqrt(N)  # Coupling strength\nkappa = 1.0  # Cavity decay rate\nn_max = 16  # Photon number cutoff\n\n# Dissipation parameters\ngamma_phi = 0.01  # Local dephasing rate\ngamma_up = 0.1  # Local pumping rate\ngamma_down = 0.1  # Local emission rate\ngamma_col_up = 0.1  # Collective pumping rate\ngamma_col_down = 0.1  # Collective emission rate\n\n# Wigner function grid\nx_min, x_max = -6, 6\np_min, p_max = -6, 6\ngrid_points = 1000\n\nprint(f\"Open Dicke Model Simulation\")\nprint(f\"=\" * 60)\nprint(f\"Parameters:\")\nprint(f\"  N = {N} (number of qubits)\")\nprint(f\"  ω₀ = {omega0}, ωc = {omega_c}\")\nprint(f\"  g = {g:.6f}\")\nprint(f\"  κ = {kappa}, nmax = {n_max}\")\nprint(f\"  Grid: {grid_points}×{grid_points} from [{x_min},{x_max}]×[{p_min},{p_max}]\")\nprint()\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef build_collective_op(single_op, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build collective operator: sum over all N qubits\n    E.g., J_z = Σᵢ σ_z^i\n    \"\"\"\n    J = None\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = single_op\n        term = qt.tensor(*op_list)\n\n        if J is None:\n            J = term\n        else:\n            J += term\n    return J\n\n\ndef build_hamiltonian():\n    \"\"\"Build the Dicke model Hamiltonian\"\"\"\n    # Cavity operators\n    a = qt.destroy(n_max)\n    a_dag = qt.create(n_max)\n\n    # Single qubit operators\n    sz = qt.sigmaz()\n    sp = qt.sigmap()\n    sm = qt.sigmam()\n\n    # Collective operators for N qubits (acting only in qubit space)\n    Jz = build_collective_op(sz, N)\n    Jp = build_collective_op(sp, N)  # J_+\n    Jm = build_collective_op(sm, N)  # J_-\n\n    # Full-system operators (cavity tensor qubit_system)\n    # Note: Jz, Jp, Jm already act in qubit space (2^N dimension)\n    # We need to tensor with cavity identity\n    a_full = qt.tensor(a, qt.qeye(2**N))\n    Jz_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jz)\n    Jp_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jp)\n    Jm_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jm)\n\n    # Hamiltonian terms\n    # H = ω₀ J_z + ωc a†a + g(a† + a)(J₊ + J₋)\n    H_qubits = omega0 * Jz_full\n    H_cavity = omega_c * a_full.dag() * a_full\n    # Interaction: g(a† + a)(J₊ + J₋) = g*a*(Jp+Jm) + g*a†*(Jp+Jm)\n    H_interaction = g * a_full * (Jp_full + Jm_full) + g * a_full.dag() * (Jp_full + Jm_full)\n\n    H = H_qubits + H_cavity + H_interaction\n\n    return H, a, sz, sp, sm, Jz, Jp, Jm\n\n\ndef build_collapse_operators(a, sz, sp, sm, Jz, Jp, Jm):\n    \"\"\"Build collapse operators for different dissipation channels\"\"\"\n\n    # Cavity loss\n    a_full = qt.tensor(a, qt.qeye(2**N))\n    c_cavity = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a_full]\n\n    # Local dephasing (all qubits)\n    c_dephasing = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sz\n        c_dephasing.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Local pumping (all qubits)\n    c_pumping = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sp\n        c_pumping.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_up) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Local emission (all qubits)\n    c_emission = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sm\n        c_emission.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_down) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Collective pumping\n    Jp_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jp)\n    c_col_pumping = [np.sqrt(gamma_col_up) * Jp_full]\n\n    # Collective emission\n    Jm_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jm)\n    c_col_emission = [np.sqrt(gamma_col_down) * Jm_full]\n\n    return {\n        \"cavity\": c_cavity,\n        \"dephasing\": c_dephasing,\n        \"pumping\": c_pumping,\n        \"emission\": c_emission,\n        \"col_pumping\": c_col_pumping,\n        \"col_emission\": c_col_emission,\n    }\n\n\ndef solve_steady_state(H, c_ops, case_name=\"\"):\n    \"\"\"Solve for steady state density matrix\"\"\"\n    print(f\"Solving steady state {case_name}...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    try:\n        # Set solver options for better convergence\n        opts = qt.Options(nsteps=10000, rtol=1e-8, atol=1e-10)\n        rho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, options=opts)\n        print(\"Done.\")\n        return rho_ss\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n\ndef extract_cavity_state(rho_ss):\n    \"\"\"\n    Extract cavity state by tracing out qubits\n    Returns reduced density matrix for cavity only\n    \"\"\"\n    # Partition: cavity (dimension n_max) + N qubits (dimension 2^N)\n    # Keep only cavity (index 0)\n    mask = [True] + [False] * N\n    rho_cavity = qt.ptrace(rho_ss, mask)\n    return rho_cavity\n\n\ndef calculate_wigner_function(rho_cavity):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Wigner function on grid\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Calculating Wigner function on {grid_points}×{grid_points} grid...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    xvec = np.linspace(x_min, x_max, grid_points)\n    pvec = np.linspace(p_min, p_max, grid_points)\n\n    W = qt.wigner(rho_cavity, xvec, pvec)\n\n    print(\"Done.\")\n    return W, xvec, pvec\n\n\ndef save_wigner_csv(W, xvec, pvec, filename):\n    \"\"\"Save Wigner function to CSV file\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Saving to {filename}...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    # Create DataFrame with p as index and x as columns\n    df = pd.DataFrame(W, index=pvec, columns=xvec)\n    df.index.name = \"p\"\n    df.columns.name = \"x\"\n\n    df.to_csv(filename, float_format=\"%.10e\")\n    print(\"Done.\")\n\n\ndef analyze_state(rho_cavity, case_num):\n    \"\"\"Analyze properties of the cavity state\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Analysis for case {case_num}:\")\n\n    # Photon number expectation value\n    a_op = qt.destroy(n_max)\n    n_expect = qt.expect(a_op.dag() * a_op, rho_cavity)\n    print(f\"    <n> = {n_expect:.6f}\")\n\n    # Purity\n    purity = qt.purity(rho_cavity)\n    print(f\"    Purity = {purity:.6f}\")\n\n    # Number of two-level systems\nomega0 = 1.0  # Two-level system frequency\nomega_c = 1.0  # Cavity mode frequency\ng = 2.0 / np.sqrt(N)  # Coupling strength\nkappa = 1.0  # Cavity decay rate\nn_max = 16  # Photon number cutoff\n\n# Dissipation parameters\ngamma_phi = 0.01  # Local dephasing rate\ngamma_up = 0.1  # Local pumping rate\ngamma_down = 0.1  # Local emission rate\ngamma_col_up = 0.1  # Collective pumping rate\ngamma_col_down = 0.1  # Collective emission rate\n\n# Wigner function grid\nx_min, x_max = -6, 6\np_min, p_max = -6, 6\ngrid_points = 1000\n\nprint(f\"Open Dicke Model Simulation\")\nprint(f\"=\" * 60)\nprint(f\"Parameters:\")\nprint(f\"  N = {N} (number of qubits)\")\nprint(f\"  ω₀ = {omega0}, ωc = {omega_c}\")\nprint(f\"  g = {g:.6f}\")\nprint(f\"  κ = {kappa}, nmax = {n_max}\")\nprint(f\"  Grid: {grid_points}×{grid_points} from [{x_min},{x_max}]×[{p_min},{p_max}]\")\nprint()\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef build_collective_op(single_op, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build collective operator: sum over all N qubits\n    E.g., J_z = Σᵢ σ_z^i\n    \"\"\"\n    J = None\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = single_op\n        term = qt.tensor(*op_list)\n\n        if J is None:\n            J = term\n        else:\n            J += term\n    return J\n\n\ndef build_hamiltonian():\n    \"\"\"Build the Dicke model Hamiltonian\"\"\"\n    # Cavity operators\n    a = qt.destroy(n_max)\n    a_dag = qt.create(n_max)\n\n    # Single qubit operators\n    sz = qt.sigmaz()\n    sp = qt.sigmap()\n    sm = qt.sigmam()\n\n    # Collective operators for N qubits (acting only in qubit space)\n    Jz = build_collective_op(sz, N)\n    Jp = build_collective_op(sp, N)  # J_+\n    Jm = build_collective_op(sm, N)  # J_-\n\n    # Build full qubit system identity (as proper tensor product)\n    qubits_id = qt.tensor(*[qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)])\n    cavity_id = qt.qeye(n_max)\n\n    # Full-system operators (cavity tensor qubit_system)\n    a_full = qt.tensor(a, qubits_id)\n    a_dag_full = qt.tensor(a_dag, qubits_id)\n    Jz_full = qt.tensor(cavity_id, Jz)\n    Jp_full = qt.tensor(cavity_id, Jp)\n    Jm_full = qt.tensor(cavity_id, Jm)\n\n    # Hamiltonian terms\n    # H = ω₀ J_z + ωc a†a + g(a† + a)(J₊ + J₋)\n    H_qubits = omega0 * Jz_full\n    H_cavity = omega_c * a_dag_full * a_full\n    # Interaction: g(a† + a)(J₊ + J₋) = g*a*(Jp+Jm) + g*a†*(Jp+Jm)\n    H_interaction = g * a_full * (Jp_full + Jm_full) + g * a_dag_full * (Jp_full + Jm_full)\n\n    H = H_qubits + H_cavity + H_interaction\n\n    return H, a, sz, sp, sm, Jz, Jp, Jm\n\n\ndef build_collapse_operators(a, sz, sp, sm, Jz, Jp, Jm):\n    \"\"\"Build collapse operators for different dissipation channels\"\"\"\n\n    # Cavity loss\n    a_full = qt.tensor(a, qt.qeye(2**N))\n    c_cavity = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a_full]\n\n    # Local dephasing (all qubits)\n    c_dephasing = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sz\n        c_dephasing.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Local pumping (all qubits)\n    c_pumping = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sp\n        c_pumping.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_up) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Local emission (all qubits)\n    c_emission = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sm\n        c_emission.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_down) * qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Collective pumping\n    Jp_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jp)\n    c_col_pumping = [np.sqrt(gamma_col_up) * Jp_full]\n\n    # Collective emission\n    Jm_full = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), Jm)\n    c_col_emission = [np.sqrt(gamma_col_down) * Jm_full]\n\n    return {\n        \"cavity\": c_cavity,\n        \"dephasing\": c_dephasing,\n        \"pumping\": c_pumping,\n        \"emission\": c_emission,\n        \"col_pumping\": c_col_pumping,\n        \"col_emission\": c_col_emission,\n    }\n\n\ndef solve_steady_state(H, c_ops, case_name=\"\"):\n    \"\"\"Solve for steady state density matrix\"\"\"\n    print(f\"Solving steady state {case_name}...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    try:\n        # Set solver options for better convergence\n        opts = qt.Options(nsteps=10000, rtol=1e-8, atol=1e-10)\n        rho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, options=opts)\n        print(\"Done.\")\n        return rho_ss\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n\ndef extract_cavity_state(rho_ss):\n    \"\"\"\n    Extract cavity state by tracing out qubits\n    Returns reduced density matrix for cavity only\n    \"\"\"\n    # Partition: cavity (dimension n_max) + N qubits (dimension 2^N)\n    # Keep only cavity (index 0)\n    mask = [True] + [False] * N\n    rho_cavity = qt.ptrace(rho_ss, mask)\n    return rho_cavity\n\n\ndef calculate_wigner_function(rho_cavity):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Wigner function on grid\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Calculating Wigner function on {grid_points}×{grid_points} grid...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    xvec = np.linspace(x_min, x_max, grid_points)\n    pvec = np.linspace(p_min, p_max, grid_points)\n\n    W = qt.wigner(rho_cavity, xvec, pvec)\n\n    print(\"Done.\")\n    return W, xvec, pvec\n\n\ndef save_wigner_csv(W, xvec, pvec, filename):\n    \"\"\"Save Wigner function to CSV file\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Saving to {filename}...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    # Create DataFrame with p as index and x as columns\n    df = pd.DataFrame(W, index=pvec, columns=xvec)\n    df.index.name = \"p\"\n    df.columns.name = \"x\"\n\n    df.to_csv(filename, float_format=\"%.10e\")\n    print(\"Done.\")\n\n\ndef analyze_state(rho_cavity, case_num):\n    \"\"\"Analyze properties of the cavity state\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Analysis for case {case_num}:\")\n\n    # Photon number expectation value\n    a_op = qt.destroy(n_max)\n    n_expect = qt.expect(a_op.dag() * a_op, rho_cavity)\n    print(f\"    <n> = {n_expect:.6f}\")\n\n    # Purity\n    purity = qt.purity(rho_cavity)\n    print(f\"    Purity = {purity:.6f}\")\n\n    # Von Neumann entropy\n    entropy = qt.entropy_vn(rho_cavity)\n    print(f\"    Entropy = {entropy:.6f}\")\n\n    return {\"photon_number\": n_expect, \"purity\": purity, \"entropy\": entropy}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Simulation\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef main():\n    # Number of two-level systems\nomega0 = 1.0  # Two-level system frequency\nomega_c = 1.0  # Cavity mode frequency\ng = 2.0 / np.sqrt(N)  # Coupling strength\nkappa = 1.0  # Cavity decay rate\nn_max = 16  # Photon number cutoff\n\n# Dissipation parameters\ngamma_phi = 0.01  # Local dephasing rate\ngamma_up = 0.1  # Local pumping rate\ngamma_down = 0.1  # Local emission rate\ngamma_col_up = 0.1  # Collective pumping rate\ngamma_col_down = 0.1  # Collective emission rate\n\n# Wigner function grid\nx_min, x_max = -6, 6\np_min, p_max = -6, 6\ngrid_points = 1000\n\nprint(f\"Open Dicke Model Simulation\")\nprint(f\"=\" * 60)\nprint(f\"Parameters:\")\nprint(f\"  N = {N} (number of qubits)\")\nprint(f\"  ω₀ = {omega0}, ωc = {omega_c}\")\nprint(f\"  g = {g:.6f}\")\nprint(f\"  κ = {kappa}, nmax = {n_max}\")\nprint(f\"  Grid: {grid_points}×{grid_points} from [{x_min},{x_max}]×[{p_min},{p_max}]\")\nprint()\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef build_collective_op(single_op, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build collective operator: sum over all N qubits\n    E.g., J_z = Σᵢ σ_z^i\n    \"\"\"\n    J = None\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = single_op\n        term = qt.tensor(*op_list)\n\n        if J is None:\n            J = term\n        else:\n            J += term\n    return J\n\n\ndef build_hamiltonian():\n    \"\"\"Build the Dicke model Hamiltonian\"\"\"\n    # Cavity operators\n    a = qt.destroy(n_max)\n    a_dag = qt.create(n_max)\n\n    # Single qubit operators\n    sz = qt.sigmaz()\n    sp = qt.sigmap()\n    sm = qt.sigmam()\n\n    # Collective operators for N qubits (acting only in qubit space)\n    Jz = build_collective_op(sz, N)\n    Jp = build_collective_op(sp, N)  # J_+\n    Jm = build_collective_op(sm, N)  # J_-\n\n    # Build full qubit system identity (as proper tensor product)\n    qubits_id = qt.tensor(*[qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)])\n    cavity_id = qt.qeye(n_max)\n\n    # Full-system operators (cavity tensor qubit_system)\n    a_full = qt.tensor(a, qubits_id)\n    a_dag_full = qt.tensor(a_dag, qubits_id)\n    Jz_full = qt.tensor(cavity_id, Jz)\n    Jp_full = qt.tensor(cavity_id, Jp)\n    Jm_full = qt.tensor(cavity_id, Jm)\n\n    # Hamiltonian terms\n    # H = ω₀ J_z + ωc a†a + g(a† + a)(J₊ + J₋)\n    H_qubits = omega0 * Jz_full\n    H_cavity = omega_c * a_dag_full * a_full\n    # Interaction: g(a† + a)(J₊ + J₋) = g*a*(Jp+Jm) + g*a†*(Jp+Jm)\n    H_interaction = g * a_full * (Jp_full + Jm_full) + g * a_dag_full * (Jp_full + Jm_full)\n\n    H = H_qubits + H_cavity + H_interaction\n\n    return H, a, sz, sp, sm, Jz, Jp, Jm\n\n\ndef build_collapse_operators(a, sz, sp, sm, Jz, Jp, Jm):\n    \"\"\"Build collapse operators for different dissipation channels\"\"\"\n\n    # Build full qubit system identity\n    qubits_id = qt.tensor(*[qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)])\n    cavity_id = qt.qeye(n_max)\n\n    # Cavity loss\n    a_full = qt.tensor(a, qubits_id)\n    c_cavity = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a_full]\n\n    # Local dephasing (all qubits)\n    c_dephasing = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sz\n        c_dephasing.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * qt.tensor(cavity_id, qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Local pumping (all qubits)\n    c_pumping = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sp\n        c_pumping.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_up) * qt.tensor(cavity_id, qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Local emission (all qubits)\n    c_emission = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sm\n        c_emission.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_down) * qt.tensor(cavity_id, qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Collective pumping\n    Jp_full = qt.tensor(cavity_id, Jp)\n    c_col_pumping = [np.sqrt(gamma_col_up) * Jp_full]\n\n    # Collective emission\n    Jm_full = qt.tensor(cavity_id, Jm)\n    c_col_emission = [np.sqrt(gamma_col_down) * Jm_full]\n\n    return {\n        \"cavity\": c_cavity,\n        \"dephasing\": c_dephasing,\n        \"pumping\": c_pumping,\n        \"emission\": c_emission,\n        \"col_pumping\": c_col_pumping,\n        \"col_emission\": c_col_emission,\n    }\n\n\ndef solve_steady_state(H, c_ops, case_name=\"\"):\n    \"\"\"Solve for steady state density matrix\"\"\"\n    print(f\"Solving steady state {case_name}...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    try:\n        # Set solver options for better convergence\n        opts = qt.Options(nsteps=10000, rtol=1e-8, atol=1e-10)\n        rho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, options=opts)\n        print(\"Done.\")\n        return rho_ss\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n\ndef extract_cavity_state(rho_ss):\n    \"\"\"\n    Extract cavity state by tracing out qubits\n    Returns reduced density matrix for cavity only\n    \"\"\"\n    # Partition: cavity (dimension n_max) + N qubits (dimension 2^N)\n    # Keep only cavity (index 0)\n    mask = [True] + [False] * N\n    rho_cavity = qt.ptrace(rho_ss, mask)\n    return rho_cavity\n\n\ndef calculate_wigner_function(rho_cavity):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Wigner function on grid\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Calculating Wigner function on {grid_points}×{grid_points} grid...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    xvec = np.linspace(x_min, x_max, grid_points)\n    pvec = np.linspace(p_min, p_max, grid_points)\n\n    W = qt.wigner(rho_cavity, xvec, pvec)\n\n    print(\"Done.\")\n    return W, xvec, pvec\n\n\ndef save_wigner_csv(W, xvec, pvec, filename):\n    \"\"\"Save Wigner function to CSV file\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Saving to {filename}...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    # Create DataFrame with p as index and x as columns\n    df = pd.DataFrame(W, index=pvec, columns=xvec)\n    df.index.name = \"p\"\n    df.columns.name = \"x\"\n\n    df.to_csv(filename, float_format=\"%.10e\")\n    print(\"Done.\")\n\n\ndef analyze_state(rho_cavity, case_num):\n    \"\"\"Analyze properties of the cavity state\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Analysis for case {case_num}:\")\n\n    # Photon number expectation value\n    a_op = qt.destroy(n_max)\n    n_expect = qt.expect(a_op.dag() * a_op, rho_cavity)\n    print(f\"    <n> = {n_expect:.6f}\")\n\n    # Purity\n    purity = qt.purity(rho_cavity)\n    print(f\"    Purity = {purity:.6f}\")\n\n    # Von Neumann entropy\n    entropy = qt.entropy_vn(rho_cavity)\n    print(f\"    Entropy = {entropy:.6f}\")\n\n    return {\"photon_number\": n_expect, \"purity\": purity, \"entropy\": entropy}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Simulation\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef main():\n    # Ensure output directory exists\n    os.makedirs(\".\", exist_ok=True)\n\n    # Build Hamiltonian\n    print(\"\\nBuilding Hamiltonian...\")\n    H, a, sz, sp, sm, Jz, Jp, Jm = build_hamiltonian()\n    print(f\"  Hilbert space dimension: {H.dims[0]}\")\n\n    # Build collapse operators\n    print(\"\\nBuilding collapse operators...\")\n    c_ops_dict = build_collapse_operators(a, sz, sp, sm, Jz, Jp, Jm)\n\n    # Define the 4 cases\n    cases = [\n        {\n            \"name\": \"Local dephasing + Local pumping\",\n            \"c_ops\": (\n                c_ops_dict[\"cavity\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"dephasing\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"pumping\"]\n            ),\n            \"file\": \"1.csv\",\n        },\n        {\n            \"name\": \"Local dephasing + Local emission\",\n            \"c_ops\": (\n                c_ops_dict[\"cavity\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"dephasing\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"emission\"]\n            ),\n            \"file\": \"2.csv\",\n        },\n        {\n            \"name\": \"Local dephasing + Local emission + Collective pumping\",\n            \"c_ops\": (\n                c_ops_dict[\"cavity\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"dephasing\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"emission\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"col_pumping\"]\n            ),\n            \"file\": \"3.csv\",\n        },\n        {\n            \"name\": \"Local dephasing + Local emission + Collective emission\",\n            \"c_ops\": (\n                c_ops_dict[\"cavity\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"dephasing\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"emission\"]\n                + c_ops_dict[\"col_emission\"]\n            ),\n            \"file\": \"4.csv\",\n        },\n    ]\n\n    # Store analysis results\n    analysis_results = []\n\n    # Process each case\n    for case_num, case in enumerate(cases, 1):\n        print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n        print(f\"Case {case_num}: {case['name']}\")\n        print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n\n        # Solve steady state\n        rho_ss = solve_steady_state(H, case[\"c_ops\"], f\"for case {case_num}\")\n        if rho_ss is None:\n            print(f\"ERROR: Failed to solve case {case_num}\")\n            continue\n\n        # Extract cavity state\n        print(f\"Extracting cavity state...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n        rho_cavity = extract_cavity_state(rho_ss)\n        print(\"Done.\")\n\n        # Analyze state properties\n        analysis = analyze_state(rho_cavity, case_num)\n        analysis[\"case\"] = case_num\n        analysis[\"name\"] = case[\"name\"]\n        analysis_results.append(analysis)\n\n        # Calculate Wigner function\n        W, xvec, pvec = calculate_wigner_function(rho_cavity)\n\n        # Verify normalization\n        integral = np.trapz(np.trapz(W, pvec, axis=0), xvec)\n        print(f\"  Wigner function integral (normalization check): {integral:.10f}\")\n\n        # Cavity operators\n    a = qt.destroy(n_max)\n    a_dag = qt.create(n_max)\n\n    # Single qubit operators\n    sz = qt.sigmaz()\n    sp = qt.sigmap()\n    sm = qt.sigmam()\n\n    # Collective operators for N qubits (acting only in qubit space)\n    Jz = build_collective_op(sz, N)\n    Jp = build_collective_op(sp, N)  # J_+\n    Jm = build_collective_op(sm, N)  # J_-\n\n    # Build full qubit system identity (as proper tensor product)\n    qubits_id = qt.tensor(*[qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)])\n    cavity_id = qt.qeye(n_max)\n\n    # Full-system operators (cavity tensor qubit_system)\n    a_full = qt.tensor(a, qubits_id)\n    a_dag_full = qt.tensor(a_dag, qubits_id)\n    Jz_full = qt.tensor(cavity_id, Jz)\n    Jp_full = qt.tensor(cavity_id, Jp)\n    Jm_full = qt.tensor(cavity_id, Jm)\n\n    # Hamiltonian terms\n    # H = ω₀ J_z + ωc a†a + g(a† + a)(J₊ + J₋)\n    H_qubits = omega0 * Jz_full\n    H_cavity = omega_c * a_dag_full * a_full\n    # Interaction: g(a† + a)(J₊ + J₋) = g*a*(Jp+Jm) + g*a†*(Jp+Jm)\n    H_interaction = g * a_full * (Jp_full + Jm_full) + g * a_dag_full * (Jp_full + Jm_full)\n\n    H = H_qubits + H_cavity + H_interaction\n\n    return H, a, sz, sp, sm, Jz, Jp, Jm\n\n\ndef build_collapse_operators(a, sz, sp, sm, Jz, Jp, Jm):\n    \"\"\"Build collapse operators for different dissipation channels\"\"\"\n\n    # Build full qubit system identity\n    qubits_id = qt.tensor(*[qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)])\n    cavity_id = qt.qeye(n_max)\n\n    # Cavity loss\n    a_full = qt.tensor(a, qubits_id)\n    c_cavity = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a_full]\n\n    # Local dephasing (all qubits)\n    c_dephasing = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sz\n        c_dephasing.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * qt.tensor(cavity_id, qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Local pumping (all qubits)\n    c_pumping = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sp\n        c_pumping.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_up) * qt.tensor(cavity_id, qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Local emission (all qubits)\n    c_emission = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        op_list = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n        op_list[i] = sm\n        c_emission.append(\n            np.sqrt(gamma_down) * qt.tensor(cavity_id, qt.tensor(*op_list))\n        )\n\n    # Collective pumping\n    Jp_full = qt.tensor(cavity_id, Jp)\n    c_col_pumping = [np.sqrt(gamma_col_up) * Jp_full]\n\n    # Collective emission\n    Jm_full = qt.tensor(cavity_id, Jm)\n    c_col_emission = [np.sqrt(gamma_col_down) * Jm_full]\n\n    return {\n        \"cavity\": c_cavity,\n        \"dephasing\": c_dephasing,\n        \"pumping\": c_pumping,\n        \"emission\": c_emission,\n        \"col_pumping\": c_col_pumping,\n        \"col_emission\": c_col_emission,\n    }\n\n\ndef solve_steady_state(H, c_ops, case_name=\"\"):\n    \"\"\"Solve for steady state density matrix\"\"\"\n    print(f\"Solving steady state {case_name}...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    try:\n        # Use iterative GMRES solver for better performance\n        rho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, method='iterative-gmres')\n        print(\"Done.\")\n        return rho_ss\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error: {e}\")\n        # Try alternative method\n        try:\n            print(f\"Retrying with eigen method...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n            rho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, method='eigen')\n            print(\"Done.\")\n            return rho_ss\n        except Exception as e2:\n            print(f\"Error: {e2}\")\n            return None\n\n\ndef extract_cavity_state(rho_ss):\n    \"\"\"\n    Extract cavity state by tracing out qubits\n    Returns reduced density matrix for cavity only\n    \"\"\"\n    # Partition: cavity (dimension n_max) + N qubits (dimension 2^N)\n    # Keep only cavity (index 0)\n    mask = [True] + [False] * N\n    rho_cavity = qt.ptrace(rho_ss, mask)\n    return rho_cavity\n\n\ndef calculate_wigner_function(rho_cavity):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Wigner function on grid\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Calculating Wigner function on {grid_points}×{grid_points} grid...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    xvec = np.linspace(x_min, x_max, grid_points)\n    pvec = np.linspace(p_min, p_max, grid_points)\n\n    W = qt.wigner(rho_cavity, xvec, pvec)\n\n    print(\"Done.\")\n    return W, xvec, pvec\n\n\ndef save_wigner_csv(W, xvec, pvec, filename):\n    \"\"\"Save Wigner function to CSV file\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Saving to {filename}...\", end=\" \", flush=True)\n\n    #
